</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Let the number of toffee $= p$
<emptyline>
As per the question,

                        &= \dfrac {6} {2 \times {6}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {6}} {2 \times \cancel {6}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

She gets an even number on the top face.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">She gets an even number on the top face.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(even number)}   &= \dfrac {3} {6} \\\\ 
                        &= \dfrac {3} {2 \times 3} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {3}} {3 \times \cancel {3}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$

even chance
</div>
</div>

<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Nina pulls one envelope from the lucky dip.

What is the probability that she has won a prize?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Nina pulls one envelope from the lucky dip.
<emptyline>
What is the probability that she has won a prize?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(card E)}        &= \dfrac {1 + 1 + 1 + 1} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {5 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \cancel {2}} {5 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {2} {5}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">%empty%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="formulacodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="formulacodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_probability
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(card E)}        &= \dfrac {1 + 1 + 1 + 1} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {4} {10} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \times 2} {5 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2 \cancel {2}} {5 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {2} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="answercodeblock_title">

<div class="answercodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="answercodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Answer Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$\dfrac {2} {5}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Find the mean average of $\pounds 1, \pounds 2, \pounds 5$ and $\pounds 10$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Find the mean average of $\pounds 1, \pounds 2, \pounds 5$ and $\pounds 10$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean average}     &= \dfrac {1 + 2 + 5 + 10} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {18} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9 \times 2} {2 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9 \cancel {2}} {2 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {9} {2}$ or $4.5$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_md_statistics_formula_mean
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean average}     &= \dfrac {1 + 2 + 5 + 10} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {18} {4} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9 \times 2} {2 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9 \cancel {2}} {2 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<ul class="subquestion lower-alpha question_type_subquestion list_type_lower-alpha">
<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If I pick one of the pupils at random. 
What is the probability that they have given the film a score of $10?$ 
Give your answer as a fraction.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If I pick one of the pupils at random. 
What is the probability that they have given the film a score of $10?$ 
Give your answer as a fraction.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(Score 10)}      &= \dfrac {3} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {5}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the mean average score out of $10$ for this group?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the mean average score out of $10$ for this group?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Mean} = \dfrac {\text{Total of items}} {\text{Number of items}}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Mean average}     &= \dfrac {10 + 8 + 10 + 10 + 7} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {45} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9 \times 5} {5} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {9 \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
                        &= 9
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Arrange the letters for each of these events in order, starting with the least likely:

- Rolling a dice and getting a three
- Rolling two dice and getting a total of $15$
- The next person who walks in the door was born on a Wednesday
- The last digit of a telephone number is NOT a $9$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Arrange the letters for each of these events in order, starting with the least likely:
<emptyline>
- Rolling a dice and getting a three
- Rolling two dice and getting a total of $15$
- The next person who walks in the door was born on a Wednesday
- The last digit of a telephone number is NOT a $9$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(3 in a dice)}                                 &= \dfrac {1} {6} \\\\
                                                      &= 0.16 \\\\
\text{P(getting a total of 15 by rolling two dice)}   &= 0 \\\\
\text{P(born on a Wednesday)}                         &= \dfrac {1} {7} \\\\
                                                      &= 0.14 \\\\
\text{P(last digit of a telephone number is NOT a 9)} &= \dfrac {9} {10} \\\\ 
                                                      &= 0.9
\end{aligned}
$

bcad
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

bcad

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>

</code></pre>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_probability
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workingcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$
\begin{aligned}

                                                      &= 0.16 \\\\
\text{P(getting a total of 15 by rolling two dice)}   &= 0 \\\\
\text{P(born on a Wednesday)}                         &= \dfrac {1} {7} \\\\
                                                      &= 0.14 \\\\
\text{P(last digit of a telephone number is NOT a 9)} &= \dfrac {9} {10} \\\\ 
                                                      &= 0.9
\end{aligned}
$

bcad

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Anna has a bag containing $8$ counters.

$4$ are red, $2$ are blue and the rest are green.

She picks a counter at random from the bag.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q26-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q26-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2013/group-1/s01-q26-i00.png")

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A fair die has six faces marked $0 \ \text{p}, 0 \ \text{p}, 0 \ \text{p}, 0 \ \text{p}, 0 \ \text{p}$ and $50 \ \text{p}$.

In a game at a village fete, players pay $10 \ \text{p}$ to roll the die 
and they win the amount shown on the top face of the die.

During the fete, the game is played $60$ times.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>

<emptyline>
In a game at a village fete, players pay $10 \ \text{p}$ to roll the die 
and they win the amount shown on the top face of the die.
<emptyline>
During the fete, the game is played $60$ times.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many times would you expect a player to win $50 \ \text{p}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many times would you expect a player to win $50 \ \text{p}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{P(50p)} &= \dfrac {1} {6}
\end{aligned}
$

Number of time die show $50\ \text{p} = 60 \times \dfrac{1}{6} = 10$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$10$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Petra has a bag containing $24$ counters which are green, blue or red.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q26-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q26-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q26-i00.png")

$50\%$ of the counters are green.

There are twice as many blue counters as red counters in the bag.


She picks one counter at random from the bag.

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q26-i01.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q26-i01.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2015/group-1/s01-q26-i01.png")


From the probability scale shown, write down the letter which represents the probability that her counter is:

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

green

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">green
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Number of green counters}     &= \dfrac{24}{2} &= 12 \\\\
\text{P(green)} &= \dfrac {12} {24} \\\\ 
                        &= \dfrac {12} {2 \times 12} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {2}} {2 \times \cancel {12}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {2}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">

<div class="question_envelope rag_pr rag_prns question">
<div class="uuid">

</li>

</div>
<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The arrow is spun and it has an equal chance of stopping at any of the numbers. Is it

- More likely to land on a prime number than not a prime number?

- Just as likely to land on a prime number as not a prime number?

- More likely to land on a number that is not a prime number rather than a prime number?

![papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png "papers/the-queens-school/sample/sample-paper-1/s01-q23-i00.png")

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The arrow is spun and it has an equal chance of stopping at any of the numbers. Is it
<emptyline>
- More likely to land on a prime number than not a prime number?
<emptyline>
- Just as likely to land on a prime number as not a prime number?
<emptyline>
- More likely to land on a number that is not a prime number rather than a prime number?
<emptyline>
%image%
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{prime numbers}         &= 2, 7, 11, 13 \\\\
\text{P(prime number)}       &= \dfrac {4} {8} \\\\  
                             &= \dfrac {4} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {\cancel {4}} {2 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\
\text{not a prime number}    &= 6, 9, 12, 4 \\\\                             
\text{P(not a prime number)} &= \dfrac {4} {8} \\\\ 
                             &= \dfrac {4} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {\cancel {4}} {2 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\                   
\end{aligned}
$

Just as likely to land on a prime number as not a prime number.
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="option" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Option**

<p>B</p>
</div>
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

Just as likely to land on a prime number as not a prime number.

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</code></pre>

<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Formulas Raw</strong>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_probability
</code></pre>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>

\begin{aligned}

                             &= \dfrac {4} {2 \times 4} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {\cancel {4}} {2 \times \cancel {4}} \\\\
                             &= \dfrac {1} {2} \\\\

<div class="answers">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the probability of rolling a **multiple of** $4$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the probability of rolling a **multiple of** $4$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{multiple of 4}           &= 4, 12, 16, 20, 24 \\\\
\text{P(multiple of 4)}       &= \dfrac {5} {6} \\\\                    
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {5} {6}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">- rtq_abbr_formula_katex_probability
</code></pre>
</div>

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What is the probability of rolling a **square number**?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What is the probability of rolling a **square number**?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
- ABBR: $\text{Probability}  = \dfrac { \text{ Favourable outcomes} } { \text{Total outcomes} }$
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{square numbers}     &= 4, 16 \\\\
\text{P(square number)} &= \dfrac {2} {6} \\\\ 
                        &= \dfrac {2} {3 \times 2} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {\cancel {2}} {3 \times \cancel {2}} \\\\
                        &= \dfrac {1} {3}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$\dfrac {1} {3}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

</code></pre>

</div>

</code></pre>

                        &= \dfrac {2} {3 \times 2} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
